AWS CloudFormation — provisions and updates infrastructure from declarative JSON or YAML templates so environments can be recreated identically. - EBS -> automates deploying code and provisioning infrastructure - CloudFormation -> Use templates to deploy code and infrastructure This item belongs to the Cloud Technology and Services domain, under Deployment.

- **Option A** ✅ (CORRECT): Correct. AWS Elastic Beanstalk — uploads your application code and automatically provisions the capacity, load balancing, scaling and health monitoring behind it.
- **Option B** ❌ (INCORRECT): AWS CodeCommit — private managed Git repositories hosted in AWS.
- **Option C** ❌ (INCORRECT): Evaluation of option C.
- **Option D** ✅ (CORRECT): Correct. AWS CloudFormation — provisions and updates infrastructure from declarative JSON or YAML templates so environments can be recreated identically.
- **Option E** ❌ (INCORRECT): AWS Config — records how resource configurations change over time and evaluates each change against rules to flag non-compliant resources.

- **D.** Amazon Simple Notification Service (Amazon SNS)

**Correct Answer:** `B`

#### Comprehensive Explanations

> Correct answer: Amazon CloudWatch. Amazon CloudWatch — collects metrics, logs and alarms describing how resources and applications are performing, and can trigger actions when a threshold is breached. - With Basic monitoring you get data on your cloudwatch metrics every 5 minutes. - Enabling detailed monitoring, you will get the data every one minute. - To check if detailed monitoring is enabled, on your EC2 Console, Select the instance, on the lower plane, Select Monitoring. This item belongs to the Cloud Technology and Services domain, under Monitoring.

- **Option A** ❌ (INCORRECT): AWS CloudTrail — records every API call in the account including who made it, from which IP address and when, which makes it the service that answers who did this.
- **Option B** ✅ (CORRECT): Correct. Amazon CloudWatch — collects metrics, logs and alarms describing how resources and applications are performing, and can trigger actions when a threshold is breached.
- **Option C** ❌ (INCORRECT): AWS Cost and Usage Report — delivers the most detailed available breakdown of charges, line by line, into an S3 bucket for custom analysis.
- **Option D** ❌ (INCORRECT): Amazon SNS — a publish and subscribe service that pushes a single message out to many subscribers such as queues, functions, email or SMS.

- **C.** AWS CodeDeploy
- **D.** Amazon Glacier
- **E.** AWS Organizations

AWS Lambda — runs code in response to events with no servers to manage, scales automatically, and bills only for the milliseconds the code executes. Amazon ECS — AWS-native container orchestration that schedules and runs Docker containers across a cluster. This item belongs to the Cloud Technology and Services domain, under Serverless compute.

- **D.** AWS Glue

This item belongs to the Cloud Technology and Services domain, under Infrastructure as code.

- **B.** AWS Direct Connect **[CORRECT]**

#### Comprehensive Explanations

AWS Direct Connect — a dedicated private physical link between a data centre and AWS that bypasses the public internet for consistent bandwidth and latency. AWS Storage Gateway — an on-premises appliance that presents local NFS, SMB or iSCSI storage while backing the data into AWS. This item belongs to the Cloud Technology and Services domain, under Hybrid networking.

- **Option B** ✅ (CORRECT): Correct. AWS Direct Connect — a dedicated private physical link between a data centre and AWS that bypasses the public internet for consistent bandwidth and latency.
- **Option C** ❌ (INCORRECT): Amazon CloudFront — a content delivery network that caches content at edge locations worldwide to cut latency and offload the origin.
- **Option D** ✅ (CORRECT): Correct. AWS Storage Gateway — an on-premises appliance that presents local NFS, SMB or iSCSI storage while backing the data into AWS.

<a id="q-293"></a>

### Question 293

**Domain:** `Domain 3: Cloud Technology and Services` | **Topic:** `Hybrid networking` | **ID:** `q0686`

> **Scenario:**  
> Which of the following allows users to provision a dedicated network connection from their internal network to AWS?

#### Options
- **A.** AWS CloudHSM
- **B.** AWS Direct Connect **[CORRECT]**
- **C.** AWS VPN
- **D.** Amazon Connect

**Correct Answer:** `B`

#### Comprehensive Explanations

> Correct answer: AWS Direct Connect. AWS Direct Connect — a dedicated private physical link between a data centre and AWS that bypasses the public internet for consistent bandwidth and latency. - AWS Direct Connect lets you establish a dedicated network connection between your network and one of the AWS Direct Connect locations. - Using industry standard 802.1q VLANs, this dedicated connection can be partitioned into multiple virtual interfaces. - This allows you to use the same connection to access public resources such as objects stored in Amazon S3 using public IP address space, and private resources such as Amazon EC2 instances running within an Amazon Virtual Private Cloud (VPC) using private IP space, while maintaining network separation between the public and private environments. - Virtual interfaces can be reconfigured at any time to meet your changing needs. This item belongs to the Cloud Technology and Services domain, under Hybrid networking.

- **Option A** ❌ (INCORRECT): AWS CloudHSM — dedicated single-tenant hardware security modules for cases where regulation requires you to hold the keys with no AWS access at all.
- **Option B** ✅ (CORRECT): Correct. AWS Direct Connect — a dedicated private physical link between a data centre and AWS that bypasses the public internet for consistent bandwidth and latency.
- **Option C** ❌ (INCORRECT): AWS Site-to-Site VPN — an encrypted tunnel between an on-premises network and a VPC over the public internet, quick to set up but subject to internet variability.
- **Option D** ❌ (INCORRECT): Amazon Connect — a cloud contact centre that can be set up without telephony hardware.

#### Comprehensive Explanations

> Correct answer: AWS Elastic Beanstalk. AWS Elastic Beanstalk — uploads your application code and automatically provisions the capacity, load balancing, scaling and health monitoring behind it. - Upload your code and Elastic Beanstalk automatically handles the deployment, from capacity provisioning, load balancing, auto-scaling to application health monitoring. - At the same time, you retain full control over the AWS resources powering your application and can access the underlying resources at any time. This item belongs to the Cloud Technology and Services domain, under Deployment.

- **Option A** ❌ (INCORRECT): AWS Config — records how resource configurations change over time and evaluates each change against rules to flag non-compliant resources.
- **Option B** ✅ (CORRECT): Correct. AWS Elastic Beanstalk — uploads your application code and automatically provisions the capacity, load balancing, scaling and health monitoring behind it.
- **Option C** ❌ (INCORRECT): Amazon Route 53 — a managed DNS service that also registers domains, performs health checks and supports latency, geolocation and failover routing.
- **Option D** ❌ (INCORRECT): Amazon CloudFront — a content delivery network that caches content at edge locations worldwide to cut latency and offload the origin.

### Question 299

- **Option B** ❌ (INCORRECT): Amazon Connect — a cloud contact centre that can be set up without telephony hardware.
- **Option C** ✅ (CORRECT): Correct. Amazon VPC — a logically isolated private network in AWS where you define the IP range, subnets, route tables and gateways.

<a id="q-300"></a>

### Question 300

**Domain:** `Domain 3: Cloud Technology and Services` | **Topic:** `Hybrid networking` | **ID:** `q0712`

> **Scenario *(Select 2)*:**  
> A company wants to migrate its applications to a VPC on AWS. These applications will need to access on-premises resources. <br/> What combination of actions will enable the company to accomplish this goal? (Choose two.)

#### Options
- **A.** Use the AWS Service Catalog to identify a list of on-premises resources that can be migrated.
- **B.** Build a VPN connection between an on-premises device and a virtual private gateway in the new VPC. **[CORRECT]**
- **C.** Use Amazon Athena to query data from the on-premises database servers.
- **D.** Connect the company's on-premises data center to AWS using AWS Direct Connect. **[CORRECT]**
- **E.** Leverage Amazon CloudFront to restrict access to static web content provided through the company's on-premises web servers.

**Correct Answer:** `B, D`

#### Comprehensive Explanations

> Correct answer: Build a VPN connection between an on-premises device and a virtual private gateway in the new VPC; Connect the company's on-premises data center to AWS using AWS Direct Connect. AWS Site-to-Site VPN — an encrypted tunnel between an on-premises network and a VPC over the public internet, quick to set up but subject to internet variability. AWS Direct Connect — a dedicated private physical link between a data centre and AWS that bypasses the public internet for consistent bandwidth and latency. This item belongs to the Cloud Technology and Services domain, under Hybrid networking.

- **Option A** ❌ (INCORRECT): AWS Service Catalog — lets an organisation publish an approved list of products that users may deploy, keeping deployments within agreed standards.
- **Option B** ✅ (CORRECT): Correct. AWS Site-to-Site VPN — an encrypted tunnel between an on-premises network and a VPC over the public internet, quick to set up but subject to internet variability.
- **Option C** ❌ (INCORRECT): Amazon Athena — runs standard SQL directly against files in Amazon S3 with no infrastructure to run, billed per terabyte scanned.
- **Option D** ✅ (CORRECT): Correct. AWS Direct Connect — a dedicated private physical link between a data centre and AWS that bypasses the public internet for consistent bandwidth and latency.
- **Option E** ❌ (INCORRECT): Amazon CloudFront — a content delivery network that caches content at edge locations worldwide to cut latency and offload the origin.

### Question 301

**Domain:** `Domain 3: Cloud Technology and Services` | **Topic:** `Hybrid networking` | **ID:** `q0715`

> **Scenario:**  
> A company requires a dedicated network connection between its on-premises servers and the AWS Cloud. <br/> Which AWS service should be used?

#### Options
- **A.** AWS VPN
- **B.** AWS Direct Connect **[CORRECT]**
- **C.** Amazon API Gateway
- **D.** Amazon Connect

**Correct Answer:** `B`

#### Comprehensive Explanations

> Correct answer: AWS Direct Connect. AWS Direct Connect — a dedicated private physical link between a data centre and AWS that bypasses the public internet for consistent bandwidth and latency. - You can use AWS Direct Connect to establish a private virtual interface from your on-premise network directly to your Amazon VPC, providing you with a private, high bandwidth network connection between your network and your VPC. - With multiple virtual interfaces, you can even establish private connectivity to multiple VPCs while maintaining network isolation. This item belongs to the Cloud Technology and Services domain, under Hybrid networking.

- **Option A** ❌ (INCORRECT): AWS Site-to-Site VPN — an encrypted tunnel between an on-premises network and a VPC over the public internet, quick to set up but subject to internet variability.

- **Option C** ❌ (INCORRECT): Amazon API Gateway — publishes, secures and throttles REST and WebSocket APIs, commonly as the front door to Lambda functions.
- **Option D** ❌ (INCORRECT): Amazon Connect — a cloud contact centre that can be set up without telephony hardware.

This item belongs to the Cloud Technology and Services domain, under Infrastructure as code.

### Question 303

**Domain:** `Domain 3: Cloud Technology and Services` | **Topic:** `Analytics` | **ID:** `q0717`

> **Scenario:**  
> Which of the following is an AWS database service?

#### Options
- **A.** Amazon Redshift **[CORRECT]**
- **B.** Amazon Elastic Block Store (Amazon EBS)
- **C.** Amazon S3 Glacier
- **D.** AWS Snowball

**Correct Answer:** `A`

#### Comprehensive Explanations

> Correct answer: Amazon Redshift. Amazon Redshift — a petabyte-scale data warehouse built for complex analytical queries and business intelligence reporting, not for transactional workloads. This item belongs to the Cloud Technology and Services domain, under Analytics.

- **Option A** ✅ (CORRECT): Correct. Amazon Redshift — a petabyte-scale data warehouse built for complex analytical queries and business intelligence reporting, not for transactional workloads.
- **Option B** ❌ (INCORRECT): Amazon EBS — network-attached block volumes for a single EC2 instance, behaving like a virtual hard disk that persists independently of the instance.
- **Option C** ❌ (INCORRECT): Amazon S3 Glacier — the lowest-cost S3 storage classes for archives, trading minutes-to-hours retrieval time for a very low price per gigabyte.
- **Option D** ❌ (INCORRECT): AWS Snowball — ruggedised physical devices shipped to your site to transfer petabyte-scale data when the network would take too long.

<a id="q-304"></a>

### Question 304

**Domain:** `Domain 3: Cloud Technology and Services` | **Topic:** `Archival storage` | **ID:** `q0723`

> **Scenario:**  
> A Cloud Practitioner needs to store data for 7 years to meet regulatory requirements. <br/> Which AWS service will meet this requirement at the LOWEST cost?

#### Options
- **A.** Amazon S3
- **B.** AWS Snowball
- **C.** Amazon Redshift
- **D.** Amazon S3 Glacier **[CORRECT]**

**Correct Answer:** `D`

#### Comprehensive Explanations

> Correct answer: Amazon S3 Glacier. Amazon S3 Glacier — the lowest-cost S3 storage classes for archives, trading minutes-to-hours retrieval time for a very low price per gigabyte. - S3 Glacier Deep Archive is Amazon S3's lowest-cost storage class and supports long-term retention and digital preservation for data that may be accessed once or twice in a year. - It is designed for customers --particularly those in highly-regulated industries, such as the Financial Services, Healthcare, and Public Sectors -- that retain data sets for 7-10 years or longer to meet regulatory compliance requirements. - S3 Glacier Deep Archive can also be used for backup and disaster recovery use cases, and is a cost-effective and easy-to-manage alternative to magnetic tape systems, whether they are on-premises libraries or off-premises services. This item belongs to the Cloud Technology and Services domain, under Archival storage.

- **Option A** ❌ (INCORRECT): Amazon S3 — durable object storage for any amount of unstructured data, with versioning, lifecycle rules and eleven nines of durability.
- **Option B** ❌ (INCORRECT): AWS Snowball — ruggedised physical devices shipped to your site to transfer petabyte-scale data when the network would take too long.
- **Option C** ❌ (INCORRECT): Amazon Redshift — a petabyte-scale data warehouse built for complex analytical queries and business intelligence reporting, not for transactional workloads.
- **Option D** ✅ (CORRECT): Correct. Amazon S3 Glacier — the lowest-cost S3 storage classes for archives, trading minutes-to-hours retrieval time for a very low price per gigabyte.

---

<a id="q-305"></a>

### Question 305

**Domain:** `Domain 3: Cloud Technology and Services` | **Topic:** `Hybrid storage` | **ID:** `q0725`

> **Scenario:**  
> What is the purpose of AWS Storage Gateway?

#### Options
- **A.** It ensures on-premises data storage is 99.999999999% durable.
- **B.** It transports petabytes of data to and from AWS.
- **C.** It connects to multiple Amazon EC2 instances.
- **D.** It connects on-premises data storage to the AWS Cloud. **[CORRECT]**

**Correct Answer:** `D`

#### Comprehensive Explanations

> Correct answer: It connects on-premises data storage to the AWS Cloud. AWS Storage Gateway — an on-premises appliance that presents local NFS, SMB or iSCSI storage while backing the data into AWS. - Moving data to the cloud is not quite as simple as flipping a switch. - For companies that have managed their own data centers or server rooms for decades, there are a few steps to consider --and it's not always wise to pull the plug on an internal infrastructure quite so quickly. - If a startup uses on-premise business servers and then experiences unexpected growth, abandoning those servers doesn't make sense (even if the long-term plan is to do exactly that). - AWS Storage Gateway is a way to bridge this gap for companies of any size. - It's a hybrid storage option that connects on-premise storage including age-old tape backup systems to the cloud in a way that also provides one console to access all storage configurations. This item belongs to the Cloud Technology and Services domain, under Hybrid storage.

- **Option A** ❌ (INCORRECT): Durability — the probability that stored data survives, which S3 achieves by replicating every object across multiple Availability Zones.
- **Option B** ❌ (INCORRECT): Evaluation of option B.
- **Option C** ❌ (INCORRECT): Amazon EC2 — resizable virtual machines where you choose the instance type, control the guest operating system, and pay per second or hour of runtime.
- **Option D** ✅ (CORRECT): Correct. AWS Storage Gateway — an on-premises appliance that presents local NFS, SMB or iSCSI storage while backing the data into AWS.

<a id="q-319"></a>

### Question 319

**Domain:** `Domain 3: Cloud Technology and Services` | **Topic:** `Hybrid networking` | **ID:** `q0778`

> **Scenario *(Select 2)*:**  
> Which AWS services provide a user with connectivity between the AWS Cloud and on-premises resources? (Choose two.)

#### Options
- **A.** AWS VPN **[CORRECT]**
- **B.** Amazon Connect
- **C.** Amazon Cognito
- **D.** AWS Direct Connect **[CORRECT]**
- **E.** AWS Managed Services

**Correct Answer:** `A, D`

#### Comprehensive Explanations

> Correct answer: AWS VPN; AWS Direct Connect. AWS Site-to-Site VPN — an encrypted tunnel between an on-premises network and a VPC over the public internet, quick to set up but subject to internet variability. AWS Direct Connect — a dedicated private physical link between a data centre and AWS that bypasses the public internet for consistent bandwidth and latency. - - This item belongs to the Cloud Technology and Services domain, under Hybrid networking.

- **Option A** ✅ (CORRECT): Correct. AWS Site-to-Site VPN — an encrypted tunnel between an on-premises network and a VPC over the public internet, quick to set up but subject to internet variability.
- **Option B** ❌ (INCORRECT): Amazon Connect — a cloud contact centre that can be set up without telephony hardware.
- **Option C** ❌ (INCORRECT): Amazon Cognito — handles sign-up, sign-in and identity federation for the end users of your web and mobile applications, not for AWS console access.
- **Option D** ✅ (CORRECT): Correct. AWS Direct Connect — a dedicated private physical link between a data centre and AWS that bypasses the public internet for consistent bandwidth and latency.
- **Option E** ❌ (INCORRECT): Evaluation of option E.

<a id="q-320"></a>

### Question 320

**Domain:** `Domain 3: Cloud Technology and Services` | **Topic:** `Relational databases` | **ID:** `q0780`

> **Scenario:**  
> A company wants to migrate a MySQL database to AWS but does not have the budget for Database Administrators to handle routine tasks including provisioning, patching, and performing backups. <br/> Which AWS service will support this use case?

#### Options
- **A.** Amazon RDS **[CORRECT]**
- **B.** Amazon DynamoDB
- **C.** Amazon DocumentDB
- **D.** Amazon ElastiCache

**Correct Answer:** `A`

#### Comprehensive Explanations

> The answer is Amazon RDS. Read the wording carefully, because this question asks for the option that does not belong. Every other option is a true statement. - Amazon RDS makes it easy to set up, operate, and scale a relational database in the cloud. - It provides cost-efficient and resizable capacity while automating time-consuming administration tasks, such as hardware provisioning, database setup, patching, and backups. - It frees you to focus on your applications, so you can give them the fast performance, high availability, security, and compatibility that they need. This item belongs to the Cloud Technology and Services domain, under Relational databases.

- **Option A** ✅ (CORRECT): Correct. Amazon RDS — managed relational databases where AWS handles provisioning, patching, backups and failover for engines such as MySQL, PostgreSQL and SQL Server.
- **Option B** ❌ (INCORRECT): Amazon DynamoDB — a serverless key-value NoSQL database with single-digit millisecond latency at any scale and no servers to manage.
- **Option C** ❌ (INCORRECT): Amazon DocumentDB — a managed document database compatible with MongoDB workloads.
- **Option D** ❌ (INCORRECT): Amazon ElastiCache — managed Redis or Memcached used to cache frequent queries in memory and take read pressure off a database.

<a id="q-321"></a>

### Question 321

**Domain:** `Domain 3: Cloud Technology and Services` | **Topic:** `Serverless compute` | **ID:** `q0788`

> **Scenario:**  
> Which AWS service is suitable for an event-driven workload?

#### Options
- **A.** Amazon EC2
- **B.** AWS Elastic Beanstalk
- **C.** AWS Lambda **[CORRECT]**
- **D.** Amazon Lumberyard

**Correct Answer:** `C`

#### Comprehensive Explanations

> Correct answer: AWS Lambda. AWS Lambda — runs code in response to events with no servers to manage, scales automatically, and bills only for the milliseconds the code executes. - An easy-to-use service for deploying and scaling web applications and web services developed in a number of programming languages. - You can configure event notifications for your Elastic Beanstalk environment so that notable events can be automatically published to an SNS topic, then pushed to topic subscribers. - As an example, you may use this event-driven architecture to coordinate your continuous integration pipeline (such as Jenkins CI). - That way, whenever an environment is created, Elastic Beanstalk publishes this event to an SNS topic, which triggers a subscribing Lambda function, which then kicks off a CI job against your newly created Elastic Beanstalk environment. This item belongs to the Cloud Technology and Services domain, under Serverless compute.

- **Option A** ❌ (INCORRECT): Amazon EC2 — resizable virtual machines where you choose the instance type, control the guest operating system, and pay per second or hour of runtime.
- **Option B** ❌ (INCORRECT): AWS Elastic Beanstalk — uploads your application code and automatically provisions the capacity, load balancing, scaling and health monitoring behind it.
- **Option C** ✅ (CORRECT): Correct. AWS Lambda — runs code in response to events with no servers to manage, scales automatically, and bills only for the milliseconds the code executes.
- **Option D** ❌ (INCORRECT): Evaluation of option D.

- **D.** Amazon CloudFront

<a id="q-333"></a>

### Question 333

**Domain:** `Domain 3: Cloud Technology and Services` | **Topic:** `Content delivery` | **ID:** `q0812`

> **Scenario:**  
> A company is running an ecommerce application hosted in Europe. To decrease latency for users who access the website from other parts of the world, the company would like to cache frequently accessed static content closer to the users. <br/> Which AWS service will support these requirements?

#### Options
- **A.** Amazon ElastiCache
- **B.** Amazon CloudFront **[CORRECT]**
- **C.** Amazon Elastic File System (Amazon EFS)
- **D.** Amazon Elastic Block Store (Amazon EBS)

**Correct Answer:** `B`

#### Comprehensive Explanations

> Correct answer: Amazon CloudFront. Amazon CloudFront — a content delivery network that caches content at edge locations worldwide to cut latency and offload the origin. - Amazon CloudFront employs a global network of edge locations and regional edge caches that cache copies of your content close to your viewers. - Amazon CloudFront ensures that end-user requests are served by the closest edge location. - As a result, viewer requests travel a short distance, improving performance for your viewers. - For files not cached at the edge locations and the regional edge caches, Amazon CloudFront keeps persistent connections with your origin servers so that those files can be fetched from the origin servers as quickly as possible. This item belongs to the Cloud Technology and Services domain, under Content delivery.

- **Option A** ❌ (INCORRECT): Amazon ElastiCache — managed Redis or Memcached used to cache frequent queries in memory and take read pressure off a database.
- **Option B** ✅ (CORRECT): Correct. Amazon CloudFront — a content delivery network that caches content at edge locations worldwide to cut latency and offload the origin.
- **Option C** ❌ (INCORRECT): Amazon EFS — a managed NFS file system that many EC2 instances across multiple Availability Zones can mount at the same time.
- **Option D** ❌ (INCORRECT): Amazon EBS — network-attached block volumes for a single EC2 instance, behaving like a virtual hard disk that persists independently of the instance.

<a id="q-359"></a>

### Question 359

**Domain:** `Domain 3: Cloud Technology and Services` | **Topic:** `Block storage` | **ID:** `q0906`

> **Scenario:**  
> Which AWS service offers persistent storage for a file system?

#### Options
- **A.** Amazon S3
- **B.** Amazon EC2 instance store
- **C.** Amazon Elastic Block Store (Amazon EBS) **[CORRECT]**
- **D.** Amazon ElastiCache

**Correct Answer:** `C`

#### Comprehensive Explanations

> Correct answer: Amazon Elastic Block Store (Amazon EBS). Amazon EBS — network-attached block volumes for a single EC2 instance, behaving like a virtual hard disk that persists independently of the instance. This item belongs to the Cloud Technology and Services domain, under Block storage.

- **Option A** ❌ (INCORRECT): Amazon S3 — durable object storage for any amount of unstructured data, with versioning, lifecycle rules and eleven nines of durability.
- **Option B** ❌ (INCORRECT): EC2 instance store — physically attached ephemeral disk that delivers very high IOPS but loses all data when the instance stops or terminates.
- **Option C** ✅ (CORRECT): Correct. Amazon EBS — network-attached block volumes for a single EC2 instance, behaving like a virtual hard disk that persists independently of the instance.
- **Option D** ❌ (INCORRECT): Amazon ElastiCache — managed Redis or Memcached used to cache frequent queries in memory and take read pressure off a database.

<a id="q-360"></a>

### Question 360

**Domain:** `Domain 3: Cloud Technology and Services` | **Topic:** `Serverless compute` | **ID:** `q0909`

#### Options

- **B.** Amazon Elasticsearch Service
- **C.** AWS Elastic Beanstalk
- **D.** Amazon DynamoDB **[CORRECT]**
- **E.** Amazon Redshift

**Correct Answer:** `A, D`

#### Comprehensive Explanations

> Correct answer: AWS Lambda; Amazon DynamoDB. AWS Lambda — runs code in response to events with no servers to manage, scales automatically, and bills only for the milliseconds the code executes. Amazon DynamoDB — a serverless key-value NoSQL database with single-digit millisecond latency at any scale and no servers to manage. This item belongs to the Cloud Technology and Services domain, under Serverless compute.

- **Option A** ✅ (CORRECT): Correct. AWS Lambda — runs code in response to events with no servers to manage, scales automatically, and bills only for the milliseconds the code executes.
- **Option B** ❌ (INCORRECT): Evaluation of option B.
- **Option C** ❌ (INCORRECT): AWS Elastic Beanstalk — uploads your application code and automatically provisions the capacity, load balancing, scaling and health monitoring behind it.
- **Option D** ✅ (CORRECT): Correct. Amazon DynamoDB — a serverless key-value NoSQL database with single-digit millisecond latency at any scale and no servers to manage.
- **Option E** ❌ (INCORRECT): Amazon Redshift — a petabyte-scale data warehouse built for complex analytical queries and business intelligence reporting, not for transactional workloads.

<a id="q-361"></a>

### Question 361

**Domain:** `Domain 3: Cloud Technology and Services` | **Topic:** `Hybrid networking` | **ID:** `q0910`

> **Scenario *(Select 2)*:**  
> Which AWS managed services can be used to extend an on-premises data center to the AWS network? (Choose two.)

#### Options
- **A.** AWS VPN **[CORRECT]**
- **B.** NAT gateway
- **C.** AWS Direct Connect **[CORRECT]**
- **D.** Amazon Connect
- **E.** Amazon Route 53

**Correct Answer:** `A, C`

#### Comprehensive Explanations

> Correct answer: AWS VPN; AWS Direct Connect. AWS Site-to-Site VPN — an encrypted tunnel between an on-premises network and a VPC over the public internet, quick to set up but subject to internet variability. AWS Direct Connect — a dedicated private physical link between a data centre and AWS that bypasses the public internet for consistent bandwidth and latency. This item belongs to the Cloud Technology and Services domain, under Hybrid networking.

- **Option A** ✅ (CORRECT): Correct. AWS Site-to-Site VPN — an encrypted tunnel between an on-premises network and a VPC over the public internet, quick to set up but subject to internet variability.
- **Option B** ❌ (INCORRECT): NAT gateway — allows instances in a private subnet to make outbound internet connections while blocking inbound connections from the internet.
- **Option C** ✅ (CORRECT): Correct. AWS Direct Connect — a dedicated private physical link between a data centre and AWS that bypasses the public internet for consistent bandwidth and latency.
- **Option D** ❌ (INCORRECT): Amazon Connect — a cloud contact centre that can be set up without telephony hardware.
- **Option E** ❌ (INCORRECT): Amazon Route 53 — a managed DNS service that also registers domains, performs health checks and supports latency, geolocation and failover routing.

> **Scenario:**  
> A company has a MySQL database running on a single Amazon EC2 instance. The company now requires higher availability in the event of an outage. <br/> Which set of tasks would meet this requirement?

#### Options
- **A.** Add an Application Load Balancer in front of the EC2 instance
- **B.** Configure EC2 Auto Recovery to move the instance to another Availability Zone
- **C.** Migrate to Amazon RDS and enable Multi-AZ **[CORRECT]**
- **D.** Enable termination protection for the EC2 instance to avoid outages

**Correct Answer:** `C`

#### Comprehensive Explanations

> Correct answer: Migrate to Amazon RDS and enable Multi-AZ. Amazon RDS — managed relational databases where AWS handles provisioning, patching, backups and failover for engines such as MySQL, PostgreSQL and SQL Server. This item belongs to the Cloud Technology and Services domain, under Relational databases.

- **Option A** ❌ (INCORRECT): Elastic Load Balancing — distributes incoming traffic across healthy targets in multiple Availability Zones and stops sending requests to instances that fail health checks.
- **Option B** ❌ (INCORRECT): Availability Zone — one or more discrete data centres inside a Region with independent power and networking, so deploying across two of them removes a single point of failure.
- **Option C** ✅ (CORRECT): Correct. Amazon RDS — managed relational databases where AWS handles provisioning, patching, backups and failover for engines such as MySQL, PostgreSQL and SQL Server.
- **Option D** ❌ (INCORRECT): Amazon EC2 — resizable virtual machines where you choose the instance type, control the guest operating system, and pay per second or hour of runtime.

- **B.** Amazon Simple Notification Service (Amazon SNS)
- **C.** Amazon Kinesis Data Streams
- **D.** Amazon Simple Queue Service (Amazon SQS) **[CORRECT]**

**Correct Answer:** `D`

#### Comprehensive Explanations

- **Option A** ❌ (INCORRECT): AWS Step Functions — coordinates multiple services into a visual state machine workflow with built-in error handling and retries.
- **Option B** ❌ (INCORRECT): Amazon SNS — a publish and subscribe service that pushes a single message out to many subscribers such as queues, functions, email or SMS.
- **Option C** ❌ (INCORRECT): Amazon Kinesis — collects and processes streaming data such as clickstreams, logs and telemetry in real time.
- **Option D** ✅ (CORRECT): Correct. Amazon SQS — a managed message queue that decouples components by letting a producer write messages that a consumer processes later.

- **B.** Amazon Simple Email Service (Amazon SES)
- **C.** Amazon CloudWatch alerts
- **D.** Amazon Simple Queue Service (Amazon SQS)

**Correct Answer:** `A`

#### Comprehensive Explanations

> Correct answer: Amazon Simple Notification Service (Amazon SNS). Amazon SNS — a publish and subscribe service that pushes a single message out to many subscribers such as queues, functions, email or SMS. This item belongs to the Cloud Technology and Services domain, under Application integration.

- **Option A** ✅ (CORRECT): Correct. Amazon SNS — a publish and subscribe service that pushes a single message out to many subscribers such as queues, functions, email or SMS.
- **Option B** ❌ (INCORRECT): Amazon SES — sends transactional and marketing email at scale.
- **Option C** ❌ (INCORRECT): Amazon CloudWatch — collects metrics, logs and alarms describing how resources and applications are performing, and can trigger actions when a threshold is breached.
- **Option D** ❌ (INCORRECT): Amazon SQS — a managed message queue that decouples components by letting a producer write messages that a consumer processes later.

<a id="q-381"></a>

### Question 381

**Domain:** `Domain 3: Cloud Technology and Services` | **Topic:** `Hybrid networking` | **ID:** `q0973`

> **Scenario:**  
> A company wants to connect to AWS over a private, low-latency connection from its remote office. <br/> What is the recommended method to meet these requirements?

#### Options
- **A.** Create a VPN tunnel
- **B.** Connect across the public internet
- **C.** Use VPC peering to create a connection.
- **D.** Use AWS Direct Connect. **[CORRECT]**

**Correct Answer:** `D`

#### Comprehensive Explanations

> Correct answer: Use AWS Direct Connect. AWS Direct Connect — a dedicated private physical link between a data centre and AWS that bypasses the public internet for consistent bandwidth and latency. This item belongs to the Cloud Technology and Services domain, under Hybrid networking.

- **Option A** ❌ (INCORRECT): AWS Site-to-Site VPN — an encrypted tunnel between an on-premises network and a VPC over the public internet, quick to set up but subject to internet variability.
- **Option B** ❌ (INCORRECT): Evaluation of option B.
- **Option C** ❌ (INCORRECT): VPC peering — a private one-to-one network link between two VPCs, which does not support transitive routing.
- **Option D** ✅ (CORRECT): Correct. AWS Direct Connect — a dedicated private physical link between a data centre and AWS that bypasses the public internet for consistent bandwidth and latency.

<a id="q-382"></a>

### Question 382

**Domain:** `Domain 3: Cloud Technology and Services` | **Topic:** `Content delivery` | **ID:** `q0982`

> **Scenario:**  
> A company is building an application that needs to deliver images and videos globally with minimal latency. <br/> Which approach can the company use to accomplish this in a cost effective manner?

#### Options
- **A.** Deliver the content through Amazon CloudFront. **[CORRECT]**
- **B.** Store the content on Amazon S3 and enable S3 cross-region replication.
- **C.** Implement a VPN across multiple AWS Regions.
- **D.** Deliver the content through AWS PrivateLink.

**Correct Answer:** `A`

#### Comprehensive Explanations

> Correct answer: Deliver the content through Amazon CloudFront. Amazon CloudFront — a content delivery network that caches content at edge locations worldwide to cut latency and offload the origin. This item belongs to the Cloud Technology and Services domain, under Content delivery.

- **Option A** ✅ (CORRECT): Correct. Amazon CloudFront — a content delivery network that caches content at edge locations worldwide to cut latency and offload the origin.
- **Option B** ❌ (INCORRECT): Amazon S3 — durable object storage for any amount of unstructured data, with versioning, lifecycle rules and eleven nines of durability.
- **Option C** ❌ (INCORRECT): AWS Region — a geographic area containing multiple isolated Availability Zones, chosen based on compliance, latency, service availability and cost.
- **Option D** ❌ (INCORRECT): VPC endpoint — lets resources in a VPC reach AWS services over the AWS private network instead of traversing the internet.

<a id="q-383"></a>

### Question 383

**Domain:** `Domain 3: Cloud Technology and Services` | **Topic:** `Networking` | **ID:** `q0985`

> **Scenario:**  
> A pharmaceutical company operates its infrastructure in a single AWS Region. The company has thousands of VPCs in a various AWS accounts that it wants to interconnect. <br/> Which AWS service or feature should the company use to help simplify management and reduce operational costs?

#### Options
- **A.** VPC endpoint
- **B.** AWS Direct Connect
- **C.** AWS Transit Gateway **[CORRECT]**
- **D.** VPC peering

**Correct Answer:** `C`

#### Comprehensive Explanations

> Correct answer: AWS Transit Gateway. AWS Transit Gateway — a central hub that connects many VPCs and on-premises networks without building a mesh of individual peering connections. This item belongs to the Cloud Technology and Services domain, under Networking.

- **Option A** ❌ (INCORRECT): VPC endpoint — lets resources in a VPC reach AWS services over the AWS private network instead of traversing the internet.
- **Option B** ❌ (INCORRECT): AWS Direct Connect — a dedicated private physical link between a data centre and AWS that bypasses the public internet for consistent bandwidth and latency.
- **Option C** ✅ (CORRECT): Correct. AWS Transit Gateway — a central hub that connects many VPCs and on-premises networks without building a mesh of individual peering connections.
- **Option D** ❌ (INCORRECT): VPC peering — a private one-to-one network link between two VPCs, which does not support transitive routing.
